# Call trees

> **Research, education and V&V only.** Not for reactor operation, licensing,
> safety decisions or emergency response.

*The exhaustive call tree of each lesson's entry points: everything inside this
workspace each one reaches within two hops, generated by
`kovan-cli code-walk-check --update` from rust-analyzer's call hierarchy
(standard library and dependencies left out). Every function is expanded once;
later calls to it are back-references. A call the tool cannot follow (a trait
method, a function pointer, a macro) is marked `UNRESOLVED`, never guessed.
The pages' own walks follow one concept path each; these trees are the
architecture map around them.*
